Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional business
and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual income
model, consistent with our Greater China Hardware Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a 7% net profit CAGR over 2026-36E and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately

value the FAU business, assuming a 40% market share in 2028.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
n Stronger-than-expected CPO volume growth
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

n Weaker-than-expected CPO volume growth

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Allen Chang

Verena Jeng 
+852-2978-1681 | verena.jeng@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
HTC Corp. (2498.TW): XR headset and AI glasses to support growth 
ahead; Near-term cost pressures remain; Sell
10 October 2026 | 12:24AM HKT
In September, HTC accelerated its global wearable expansion with the commercial 
launch of the new round-frame edition of its VIVE Eagle AI smart glasses alongside 
substantial software enhancements across North America, Europe, and Australia 
(news). Features include hands-free image capture, first-person POV live streaming, 
real-time AI translation, AI Notes, dashcam utility, and assistive vision for the visually 
impaired. Meanwhile, the company continues to anchor its VR headset strategy 
around commercial enterprise solutions, underscored by its flagship deployment 
with Axon which shifts hazardous tactical training into virtual environments to 
reduce operational costs, complemented by use cases in complex surgical 
simulation, interior space visualization, and virtual meeting rooms. 
While we maintain our constructive view on HTC’s long-term growth trajectory 
backed by sustained product innovation, we are Sell rated given near-term earnings 
headwinds - specifically core monetization drivers across XR/ VR/ AI glasses and 
software will require extended lead time to ramp adoption, compounded by 
consumer electronics demand softness and margin compression from elevated 
memory component costs. We would turn more positive if we see 
stronger-than-expected XR headset/ AI glasses growth driven by market demand, 
application development or customer expansion.  
Earnings revisions: We factor in 3Q26 revenues and revise down 2026-28E net 
income. We revise down 2026-28E revenues by 4%/ 0%/ 27% due to demand 
pressures of consumer electronics (impacted by soaring memory cost) and soft 
demand of XR products in the near-term. GM estimates are largely unchanged in 
2026-2027E, and we revise down our OPM estimates by 2.1/ 0.3ppts on a smaller 
business scale. GM are revised up by 5.7ppts in 2028E to reflect mix upgrade to VR 
hardware and software with high margin.

### Page 2

Valuation: With earnings revisions and an unchanged valuation methodology, our 12M 
target price is revised down to NT$32.8 (vs. previously at NT$34.4). We continue to 
derive our target price via 85% based on a fundamental valuation and 15% from a 
theoretical M&A valuation. (1) Our fundamental valuation is based on 2028E P/E of 
19.3x (unchanged), discounted back to 2026E at an 11.5% COE. (2) Our M&A valuation is 
based on 23.6x 2028E P/E (unchanged) then discounted back to 2026E at an 11.5% 
COE. 
Our target 2028E discounted P/E multiple of 19.3x is in line with XR peers’ historical avg. 
P/E at 19.3x in 2022 (method unchanged, Exhibit 5). HTC’s 2029 NI growth will be 15% 
YoY per our estimate, which is lower than XR peers’ (Meta/ Goertek/ Microsoft/ Apple/ 
Sony) 2023-24 avg. NI growth at 23% YoY. Our M&A target multiple is 23.6x 2028E P/E, 
which is derived from the peers’ historical avg. +1std P/E in 2022 (methodology 
unchanged). We use avg. +1std P/E of peers to reflect the M&A premium.  
Our 12M target price of NT$32.8 implies 4.1x 2026E P/S vs. 126% revenue YoY growth 
on average in 2026E-27E. The implied 2026E P/S of 4.1x is lower than the company’s 
current price implied 2026E P/S at 4.9x, showing that valuation is stretched. Maintain 
Sell.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - HTC Corp. 
Valuation methodology: We are Sell rated on HTC with a 12M target price of NT$32.8, 
which is derived 85% from a fundamental valuation and 15% from a theoretical M&A 
valuation. (1) Our fundamental valuation is based on 19.3x 2028E P/E, discounted back 
to 2026E at an 11.5% COE. The 19.3x target P/E multiple is derived from peers’ avg. P/E 
in 2022; (2) Our M&A valuation is based on 23.6x 2028E P/E and discounted back to 
2026E at an 11.5% COE. 
Key risks: 1) Better-than-expected VR demand; 2) lower-than-expected competition in 
VR headsets; and 3) lower-than-expected price pressure from peers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 2

For the month of October 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will decrease 
to Rmb729mn (-10% MoM, +16% YoY)/NT$3,447mn (-10% MoM, +28% YoY) 
We expect October revenue will go down by 10% MoM to Rmb729mn/NT$3,447mn, 
mainly due to 19% fewer working days in Mainland China. However, we expect to see 
improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb32.3mn in Oct vs. Rmb 29.4mn in Sept) as 
the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key 
customers, including electronics, followed by machine tools and battery, and we further 
expect demand to improve following the end of low season.

Earnings and TP revisions 
We revise down our 2026E earnings estimates by 1% to factor in actual 3Q26 revenue 
(revise down 2026 revenue by 1%). We revise down our 2027E/2028E earnings estimate 
by 1%/1% to factor in a more conservative assumption on battery and linear guide 
business (revise down AirTAC revenue by 1%/1% in 2027E/2028E). We continue to see 
the company focusing on cost control as well as experiencing a better product mix 
outlook, but we expect some offset by rising material cost (revise down 2026E/2028E 
OPM by 0.1/0.1ppt).

### Page 3

Maintain our Buy rating on AirTAC, with a new 12m TP of NT$1,895 (from NT$1,925), 
based on an unchanged target P/E of 26x on 2027 EPS.

Investment view: Accelerating monthly YoY from smaller segments could be a surprise. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC as we continue to believe the company is a key 
beneficiary of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in 
mainland China, and we expect the company will also benefit from the prolonged 
upcycle supported by the ongoing government stimulus policies. We see multiple key 
end-segments’ performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 
20%+ YoY growth (see here), with battery/textile/machine tools/electronics delivering 
55%/46%/41%/23% YTD YoY growth while total revenue in Rmb is tracking at 23% YTD 
YoY growth. 
We continue to see solid monthly YoY growth from smaller segments, such as energy 
lighting and textile. However, the battery segment, which is the key growth driver in 
2025 (with 89% YoY in 2025), is seeing its first negative monthly YoY growth since Oct 
2024, while other major contribution sectors, such as electronics and automotive are 
seeing decelerating monthly YoY growth. As a result, we remain cautious on the 
potential of exaggerated orders (including advance or duplicate orders) despite new 
orders intake continuing to outpace shipments, and we will continue to monitor the 
underlying end-demand closely.

### Page 4

Maintain Buy on AirTAC with a lower 12m TP of NT$1,895 (from NT$1,925). 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,895 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 12 Oct 2026 close.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Nanya Technology Corp.
(2408.TW) From To
Price Target NT$580.00 NT$600.00
DRAM supply constraints continue. Contract renewals drove
Nanya's 3Q beat.  CXMT's G5 milestone strengthens its tech
roadmap, but yield ramp and customer qualification take time.

Nanya 3Q beat; LTAs in spotlight:  Nanya reported 3Q revenue of NT$134bn, +62%
Q/Q, +612% Y/Y, 8% above consensus. 3Q gross profit was NT$114bn, +114% Q/Q,
12% above consensus, implying a GM of 85.7%. 3Q net profit was NT$88bn, +75%
Q/Q, 9% above consensus, implying a net margin of 65.1%. The 3Q Q/Q revenue
growth was driven mainly by ASP increase, which primarily reflects renewal of the
one-year and six-month L TAs, which were previously negotiated when ASPs were

lower; most  expired in 3Q26. Currently L TAs cover 50-70% of output; only 20% of
output is covered by three-year contracts; Nanya intends to extend the L TA
durations for longer-term supply according to customer demand, and there are
discussions on price floors and ceilings for the new contracts.
Nanya management sees continued DRAM shortage in 4Q and expects that ASP
may continue to improve: The supply constraint is the long lead time for new
capacity, and the undersupply is unlikely to be resolved in the near term. Nanya

targets capex  of NT$69.7bn in 2026 and NT$346bn  for the 35.9kwpm capacity of its
new Fab 5A from 2026-29. Management estimates that AI infra currently accounts
for ~20% of its revenue mix, but that could increase to 40-50% by 2029/30.
CXMT updates: On September 7 during Q&A, CXMT reiterated expectations for
tight DRAM supply in 2H26; its response on Apple expressed openness to global
customers without confirming a supply agreement. On September 21, after its G5

mass production announcement, CXMT clarified that G5 sales are not yet at scale,
while yield ramp-up and customer qualification would take time. Based on our
checks, CXMT's HBM3E yield is already 40%, and DRAM wafer input should ramp to
30kwpm by end-2027.
Constrained DRAM supply is the industry consensus: The 4QF26 earnings call for
Micron  (covered by Joseph Moore) highlighted that  persistent supply constraints are
expected through CY2027-28. It cited industry-wide clean room constraints owing to

lengthy construction; most of the increase in Micron's F27 capex (vs. its previous
plan) is allocated to construction, targeting clean room availability in late CY28 and
beyond. Micron also expects rising industry HBM wafer requirements to limit bit
growth, forecasting industry DRAM bit shipment growth of low-20s (%) in CY27-28.
Samsung (covered by Shawn Kim) also expects HBM to absorb nearly 30% of
industry DRAM wafer capacity in 2027, vs ~20% currently.

Stock implications:
• Nanya Tech (OW):  Price target raised to NT$600; 2026e, 2027e, and 2028e
EPS raised  22%, 18%, and 19%, respectively.
•  CXMT (OW): Base case value/price target unchanged at Rmb88.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Nanya Tech: Valuation Methodology
We raise our price target (base case scenario value) from NT$580 to NT$600: Our
price target reflects a BVPS multiple of 4.11x for 2026 (was 4.77x) versus ROE of 76% for
2026.  We lower our target multiple to reflect recent memory segment volatility. Our
2026e BVPS estimates is raised to NT$146.00 from NT$121.52. We expect DDR4 pricing
strength and L TAs to drive a re-rating.
We raise our bull and bear case values from NT$1,230 and NT$320 to NT$1,270 and NT

$330, respectively, implying 8.70x and 2.26x 2026e BVPS.

Morgan Stanley Research 11
CXMT: Valuation Methodology
We keep our base case value/price target unchanged: We continue to apply RI
methodology to derive our price target. The price target did not change despite our
2026/27 earnings estimate increases because the contribution to calculated value from
our RI model comes mostly from the subsequent forecast period  and the continuing value.
Key RI assumptions remain unchanged, including cost of equity of 9.9%, , intermediate

growth rate of 11.0%, terminal growth rate of 3.0%, and cash dividend payout ratio of 51%,
.
Our base case value/price target now implies 14.3x 2027e EPS vs. our prior target multiple
of 18.4x 2027e BVPS, as we see a stronger DRAM market outlook given the continued
supply constraint; we also believe the G5 breakthrough reduces risks around CXMT's
technology roadmap.
Our bull case value remains unchanged at Rmb132; it now implies 21.5x our 2027e EPS.

We adjust our bear case value to Rmb40 from Rmb70 to account for recent volatility; it
now implies 6.5x 2027e EPS.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

公司簡介 
樺漢為全球工業電腦大廠 ， 為鴻海轉投資工
業電腦廠，產品包括 POS(point of sale)系
統、ATM 系統、工業控制系統、車載系統、
人機互動式產品和其他周邊如處理器 、 記憶
體等產品 。 主要分成三大事業群 ： 工業物聯
網、智慧軟體與方案、智慧工廠與廠務。
2026 年 1-9 月營收占比：工業物聯網
45.7%、 智慧工廠與廠務42.6％ 、 智慧軟體
與方案 11.7%。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

張智皓 chihhao.chang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

樺漢 (6414 TT) 
在手訂單維持 2550 億元

永豐觀點 
三大事業體持續成長，第三季營收同期新高，未來營運將聚焦於 Physical AI 市場
發展。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係考量(1) 實體 AI 應用落地，包含 AMR、自動駕駛、智慧
工廠、機器人等應用，樺漢從產品、平台、軟硬整合延伸，潛在市場成長空間相當
大；(2) Kontron 完成整併，策略整合綜效逐漸顯現，擴大競爭優勢；(3) 除三大
事業體接單動能穩健外， 智能零售市場成長性高，樺漢將從硬體製造跨足到軟體平
台 ， 目前合作對象皆外國內外知名餐飲及零售商等 。 評價方面 ， 以2027 年稅後 EPS 
28.5 元，目前本益比約 14 倍，過往歷史本益比區間為 13~22 倍，考量在手訂單
持續墊高，營運規模效益可期，故維持買進的投資建議，目標價485 元(17X 2027 
EPS)。

ESG 評析 
樺漢企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 A 等。

### Page 2

2

樺漢為全球工業電腦大廠：樺漢科技股份有限公司成立於 1999 年，為鴻海轉投資
工業電腦廠，產品包括 POS(point of sale)系統、ATM 系統、工業控制系統、車載
系統 、 人機互動式產品和其他周邊如處理器 、 記憶體等產品 。 最大股東為鴻海集團 ，
持有 24.1%股權(包括寶鑫國際 22.7%、鴻揚創投 1.4%)。樺漢主要分成三大事業
群：工業物聯網、智慧軟體與方案、智慧工廠與廠務。2026 年 1-9 月營收占比：
工業物聯網 45.7%、智慧工廠與廠務 42.6％、智慧軟體與方案 11.7%。 
邊緣運算及半導體應用， 3Q26 營收單季新高 ：樺漢 3Q26 營收 496.4 億元
(+2.6%QoQ，+47.4%YoY)， 成長主係來自於工業物聯網中邊緣運算需求強勁以及
智慧工廠受惠於半導體客戶廠務工程進度持續推薦 ， 智慧軟體與方案部門因整併及
專 案 進 度 影 響 略 微 衰 退， 研 究 部 預 估 在 產 品 組 合 差 異 不 大 下 ， 毛 利 率
19.5%(+0.2pts QoQ，-2.2ptsYoY)，營利率 5.9%(+0.3ptsQoQ，+2.1ptsYoY)，
稅後淨利 10.35 億元(-1.5%QoQ，+8.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 6.59 元。 
訂單能見度佳，2026 年高成長不變：展望第四季，樺漢維持全年高成長目標，三
大事業體出貨狀況良好，其中 NCRV 全年預估貢獻 160 億元，Kontron 亦完成收
購並持續深化營運綜效，智慧工廠業務訂單將逐季認列營收。研究部預估樺漢
4Q26 營收 519.9 億元(+4.7%QoQ，+31.6%YoY)，毛利率 19.6%(+0.1ptsQoQ，
-1ptsYoY)，營利率 6.7%(+0.8ptsQoQ，-1.2ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 11.37 億元
(+9.8%QoQ，+30.6%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 7.24 元。預估 2026 年營收 1871.3 億
元 (+31.5%YoY)， 受 產 品 組 合 影 響 ， 毛 利 率19.6%(-0.9pts YoY) ， 營 利 率
6%(+1.4pts YoY)，稅後淨利 39.75 億元(+23.7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 25.32 元。 
在手訂單 2550 億元，朝向成為高毛利軟硬整合系統商：樺漢集團策略朝向聚焦高
毛利高成長事業，如從 Physical AI 相關產業進行全面整合，以成為系統整合商為
營運重點，目前在手訂單維持 2550 億高檔並持續推升。此外，持續整合Kontron
營運創造縱效，透過技術整合及市場聚焦，管理層目標 Kontron2030 年為營收達
26 億歐元、EBITDA 目標 4.2 億歐元，並透過持續降低供應鏈採購成本及製造成
本，擴大營運槓桿優勢，提高軟體等高價值產品。獲利方面，預估高毛利產品營收
占比將持續提升，產業聚焦於零售餐飲、智慧能源與樓宇、交通、醫療長照、智慧
城市與公共安全、智慧製造等。展望未來，預估 2027 年 NCRV 營收年增 15%，
智慧零售將成為下一個成長動能 ，樺漢將打造平台的生台戲 ， 從方案代理商製產品
銷售並與 Nvidia 及聯發科技術合作。長期來看，無論是透過策略合作或者併購，

樺漢在供應鏈、生產和管理成本上都將達到合作的綜效；智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)方

體營收，優化獲利能力。另一方面，實體 AI 市場持續擴大中，樺漢已有許多專案

## Document 193: 9月和三季度经济数据点评：内需出现向好迹象

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月和三季度经济数据点评：内需出现向好迹象.pdf
Report source: 中银证券
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

宏观经济 | 证券研究报告 — 总量点评 2026 年 10 月 19 日

相关研究报告 
《策略周报》20261018 
《宏观和大类资产配置周报》20261018 
《中期选举，AI 叙事，美伊局势》20261018

中银国际证券股份有限公司 
具备证券投资咨询业务资格 
宏观经济

证券分析师：张晓娇 
xiaojiao.zhang@bocichina.com 
证券投资咨询业务证书编号：S1300514010002

证券分析师：朱启兵 
(8610)66229359 
Qibing.Zhu@bocichina.com 
证券投资咨询业务证书编号：S1300516090001

9 月和三季度经济数据点评

内需出现向好迹象   
9 月经济数据中，固投累计同比增速和社零当月同比增速均好于万得一致预期，工业
增加值当月同比增速符合万得一致预期且维持在相对较高水平。 
◼ 2026 年三季度 GDP 实际同比增速 4.5%，三季度名义 GDP 同比增速 6.0%；前三
季度实际 GDP 累计同比增长 4.6%，名义 GDP 累计同比增长 5.6%。 
◼ 9 月工业增加值同比增长 5.5%。1-9 月采矿业工业增加值累计同比增速 2.0%，制
造业累计同比增速 5.7%，公用事业累计同比增速 5.4%，高技术产业累计同比增
速 14.3%。 
◼ 9 月社零同比增长 1.1%， 其中， 除汽车以外的消费品零售额同比增长3.9%。 限额
以上商品消费中，算力消费品依旧主导社零，金价下跌推动金银珠宝消费，房地
产后周期消费短期未见好转。 
◼ 1-9 月固定资产投资累计同比增速下降 7.2%，民间固定资产投资累计同比下降
10.4%， 扣除房地产开发投资，1-9 月全国固定资产投资下降 4.2%， 民间投资下降
6.7%。 分主要行业看， 制造业投资1-9 月累计同比下降 2.1%， 基建投资累计同比
下降 6.1%，地产投资累计同比下降 20.6%。 
◼ 1-9 月住宅投资增速下降 20.5%， 商品房销售面积同比下降13.2%， 商品房销售额
累计同比下降 13.9%。2026 年 9 月，70 个大中城市新建商品住宅价格指数同比下
降 3.0%，较 8 月上升 0.3 个百分点，二手住宅价格指数下降 4.8%，较 8 月上升
0.3 个百分点。 
◼ 2026 年前三季度全国居民人均可支配收入 34230 元，同比增长 5.3%，居民人均
消费支出 22354 元，同比增长 2.7%。2026 年前三季度居民边际消费倾向较上半
年小幅上升，但整体来看居民消费意愿较弱，边际消费倾向和增量收入边际消费
倾向对比 2025 年同期均有所下降，特别是增量收入边际消费倾向降幅较大。 
◼ 三季度经济增速超预期，全年稳增长压力下降。三季度实际 GDP 同比增长 4.5%
超出市场预期，带动前三季度实际 GDP 累计同比增速达到 4.6%，略高于全年增
速目标区间下限，加之去年四季度经济增长基数偏低，一定程度上缓解了短期稳
增长的压力。从 9 月经济数据看，稳增长的政策起到了一定效果：工业增加值同
比增速符合市场预期，同时维持在较高增速上；社零同比增速较 8 月明显上升，
表明内需仍有平稳释放的潜力；固定资产投资累计同比降幅持平于 1-8 月，但从
我们计算的当月增速看， 基建投资和制造业投资增速均较8 月出现较大幅度好转。
从季节规律看，四季度经济增长仍有支撑：一是生产端补库存，可能驱动工业增
加值同比增速维持在当前的增速水平，考虑到 PPI 同比增速走高和 9 月出口交货
值增速走高，价格因素和外需可能成为四季度生产端超预期的推动力；二是四季
度是传统消费旺季，从社零环比增速看，在 9 月环比高增长的基础上，四季度的
三个月在电商促销和春节前消费等因素推动下，环比仍能维持较强的正增长；三
是四季度也是海外消费旺季，进出口或依赖我国生产端优势，持续受益于外需拉
动。 
◼ 经济向“新”带动政策求“变”。 8 月底至今，从稳增长的角度出发，政策做了
多方面调整：一是国家发改委等部门召开全国投资工作推进会议，部署七项稳投
资工作，二是住建部等三部门推进商品住房销售制度改革，稳定地产预期，三是
证监会发文支持构建房地产发展新模式，四是央行调整完善几项货币政策工具稳
增长， 五是财政部安排使用地方政府债务结存限额5500 亿元， 六是中共中央、 国
务院印发《关于发展新质生产力的意见》，继续大力支持经济转型升级等等。我
们认为虽然 2026 年年内稳增长的压力减轻， 但从中长期看， 经济增长的挑战依然
很大： 从全球范围内看， 包括但不限于AI 技术发展对生产关系的冲击、 地缘局势
变化对能源和通胀的影响、 经济增长K 型分化在行业之间和国别之间带来的发展
不均衡、各经济体面临的增长挑战与财政压力、发达国家货币政策收缩与金融条
件收紧对全球资本市场的外溢性影响等等；从国内看，我们面临的挑战依然是以
维持经济向新向优趋势和供强需弱的现实矛盾为主。我们认为未来一段时间内，
我国宏观经济的主要表现或仍是增速缓步下台阶，因此积极的财政政策和适度宽
松的货币政策发力重点或更多聚焦于托底民生和实现社会公平，其次是发展先进
生产力，然后才是稳增长。尽管国内经济看起来只是“平稳”，但我们认为相较
于其他经济体，我国的比较优势依然非常明显。 
◼ 风险提示：全球通胀二次上行；欧美经济超预期回落；国际局势复杂化。

### Page 2

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 2

2026 年三季度实际 GDP 同比增长 4.5%  
2026 年三季度 GDP 实际同比增速 4.5%，较2026 年二季度增速上升 0.2 个百分点； 三季度名义GDP
同比增速 6.0%，较 2026 年二季度增速上升 0.1 个百分点。前三季度实际 GDP 累计同比增长 4.6%，
名义 GDP 累计同比增长 5.6%。 
前三季度平减指数累计同比上升 0.99%,较上半年平减指数累计同比上升 0.29 个百分点。三季度平减
指数上升，主要还是受 PPI 同比增速处于较高水平的拉动。

图表 1. GDP 同比增速

资料来源：万得，中银证券

三季度第二产业和第三产业实际 GDP 当季同比增速均较二季度有所上升 。 
分产业看 ，2026 年三季度第一产业实际 GDP 当季同比增长 3.6%， 增速较二季度下降0.1 个百分点 ，
第二产业实际 GDP 当季同比增长 3.4%，增速较二季度上升 0.4 个百分点，第三产业实际 GDP 当季
同比增长 5.2%，增速较二季度上升 0.1 个百分点。 
从三次产业对 GDP 累计同比贡献率看，2026 年前三季度,第三产业的贡献率为 65.9%,较上半年下降
0.2 个百分点，其次是第二产业的贡献率 29.5%,较上半年下降 0.7 个百分点，较上半年贡献率上升的
主要是第一产业，贡献率 4.6%，上升 0.9 个百分点；与 2023 年-2025 年三次产业的贡献率平均水平
相比，2026 年前三季度第三产业对 GDP 累计同比的贡献率偏高 4.0 个百分点，第二产业贡献率偏低
3.8 个百分点。

### Page 3

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 3

工业增加值同比增速维持在较高水平 
9 月工业增加值同比增长 5.5%，较 8 月上升 0.3 个百分点，较去年同期下降 1.0 个百分点，基本持平
于万得一致预期的 5.52%。

图表 4. 工业增加值同比增速

资料来源：万得，中银证券

从环比增速来看，9 月工业增加值环比上升 0.61%，与 2016 年-2019 年期间同期平均水平 0.56%相比
偏高,略低于去年同期的环比增速 0.65%，9 月工业增加值环比较 8 月上行,符合季节性规律。 
从行业分类来看，1-9 月采矿业工业增加值累计同比增速 2.0%，制造业累计同比增速 5.7%，公用事
业累计同比增速 5.4%， 高技术产业累计同比增速14.3%。 从企业类型来看 ，1-9 月国企工业增加值同
比增速 3.8%，股份制企业同比增速 5.8%，外企工业增加值同比增速 3.2%，私企工业增加值同比增
速 4.3%。与 1-8 月相比，1-9 月累计同比增速上升的是公共事业、高技术产业和外企,下降的是采矿
业和私企。9 月当月工业增加值同比增速相较 8 月，各行业和企业类型中工业增加值同比增速上升
的是采矿业、公共事业、国企、股份制、外企和私企，下降的是高技术产业和制造业 。 
1-9 月，装备制造业增加值同比增长 10.2%，高技术制造业增加值增长 14.3%，分别快于全部规模以
上工业增加值 4.9 和 9.0 个百分点；分产品看，3D 打印设备、锂离子电池、工业机器人产品产量同
比分别增长 62.5%、39.5%、29.1%。

### Page 4

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 4

9 月，采矿业和大宗周期品加工业当月工业增加值同比增速较 8 月增速相对较强，但受上游原材料
涨价影响，中游设备制造业工业增加值同比增速表现偏弱 。 
采矿业方面，1-9 月煤炭开采和洗选业增加值累计同比增长 0.2%，石油和天然气开采业增加值累计
同比增长 5.7%；从当月同比增速来看，较 8 月增速上升幅度均超过 2.0 个百分点。 
消费类制造业方面，1-9 月农副食品加工业增加值累计同比增长 4.6%，纺织业增加值累计同比增长
3.2%，医药制造业增加值累计同比增长 5.2%；从当月同比增速来看，较 8 月增速下降的是农副食品
加工业和医药制造业。 
大宗周期品加工业方面，1-9 月化学原料及化学制品制造业增加值累计同比增长 3.6%，非金属矿物
制品业增加值累计同比下降 2.9%， 黑色金属冶炼及压延加工业增加值累计同比增长1.7%， 有色金属
冶炼及压延加工业增加值累计同比下降 1.6%；从当月同比增速来看，较 8 月增速下降的仅有有色金
属，化学原料和非金属矿物当月工业增加值增速均较 8 月升幅超过 1.0 个百分点。 
中游设备制造业方面，1-9 月通用设备制造业增加值累计同比增长 8.2%，专用设备制造业增加值累
计同比增长 9.5%， 汽车制造业增加值累计同比增长7.3%， 电气机械及器材制造业增加值累计同比增
长 7.1%，计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增加值累计同比增长 15.8%；从当月同比增速来看，
较 8 月增速上升的仅有专用设备，汽车制造、电气设备和通用设备增速较 8 月降幅均超过 1.0 个百
分点。

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 5

9 月外需延续较强走势，出口交货值同比大幅上升，产销率小幅上升。 
9 月规模以上工业企业产销率为 97.0%，较 8 月上升 0.2 个百分点，较去年同期上升 0.3 个百分点，
当月同比增速下降 0.2%；1-9 月规模以上工业企业产销率为 96.4%，较去年同期上升 0.5 个百分点，
累计同比增长 0.1 个百分点。 
9 月规模以上工业企业出口交货值同比增长 17.0%，较 8 月上升 5.9 个百分点，1-9 月规模以上工业
企业出口交货值累计同比增长 10.7%，较 1-8 月上升 0.7 个百分点。

社零增速明显上升

较 8 月上升 1.4 个百分点；1-9 月社零累计同比增长 1.1%，其中，服务消费累计同比增长 5.0%，较
1-8 月上升 0.1 个百分点。 
9 月社零环比增长 0.4%，虽然较历史同期平均水平明显偏低，但较去年同期环比增速上升 0.5 个百

1-9 月网上商品和服务零售额累计同比增长 4.4%，增速较 1-8 月下降 0.2 个百分点，其中实物商品网
上零售额累计同比增长 3.8%， 增速较1-8 月下降 0.5 个百分点 。1-9 月网上零售额累计占比为 40.9%，
较 2025 年同期上升 10.1 个百分点，较 1-8 月下降 0.2 个百分点。 
9 月商品消费同比增长 1.0%，较 8 月上升 0.7 个百分点，限额以上商品消费同比下降 2.5%，较 8 月
上升 1.5 个百分点；9 月餐饮消费同比增长 2.1%，较 8 月上升 1.0 个百分点，限额以上餐饮消费同比
增长 2.0%，较 8 月上升 2.7 个百分点。前三季度，在服务零售额中，通讯信息服务类、旅游咨询租
赁服务类、文体休闲服务类零售额较快增长。 
1-9 月限额以上企业消费品零售总额累计同比下降 1.5%， 限额以上企业商品消费累计同比下降1.8%,
弱于餐饮消费累计同比增速 1.3%。

9 月算力消费品依旧主导社零，金价下跌推动金银珠宝消费，房地产后周期消费短期未见好转 。 
从产品分类来看，9 月限额以上企业商品零售总额增速较高的细分行业包括通讯器材类（31.1%）、
烟酒类（13.8%）和文化办公用品类（7.9%），增速较低的细分行业包括汽车类（-21.2%）、金银珠
宝类 （-9.6%） 和建筑及装潢材料类 （-9.2%） ；与8 月相比，增速上升较多的有金银珠宝类、石油及
制品类和家具类，增速下降的仅有汽车类和家用电器。

### Page 8

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 8

图表 21. 固定资产投资环比增速的季节规律  图表 22. 固投各项构成分类的累计同比 增速

资料来源：万得，中银证券  资料来源：万得，中银证券

9 月制造业和基建当月投资增速较 8 月开始回升。 
从产业分类来看，第一产业累计同比下降 3.9%，第二产业累计同比下降 2.7%，第三产业累计同比下
降 10.0%； 从地区来看 ， 东部累计同比下降10.3%， 中部下降11.1%， 西部下降8.7%， 东北下降26.0%；
从企业类型来看，内资企业累计同比下降 7.0%，国有控股同比下降 3.2%，外商投资下降 6.7%。 
分主要行业看，制造业投资 1-9 月累计同比下降 2.1%，基建投资累计同比下降 6.1%，地产投资累计
同比下降 20.6%。从我们测算的当月同比增速看，9 月制造业投资同比增速下降 0.9%，较 8 月上升
5.1 个百分点，基建投资同比增速下降 10.4%，较 8 月上升 4.3 个百分点，房地产投资同比增速下降
27.2%，较 8 月下降 1.0 个百分点。 
1-9 月固投增速较高的一级分类行业包括采矿业（3.2%）、交运仓储（1.9%）和制造业（-2.1%），
固投增速较低的行业包括文体娱（-17.0%） 、教育（-15.3%）和卫生社会（-14.0%） 。制造业行业分
类下，固投增速较高的细分行业包括铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业（15.8%） 、计算
机、通信和其他电子设备制造业（9.6%）和纺织业（4.2%），固投增速较低的行业包括食品制造业
（-10.3%）、金属制品业（-10.1%）和专用设备制造业（-8.9%）。 
与 1-8 月相比，一级分类行业中固投增速上行的行业是教育、制造业和公共事业，固投增速下行较
多的行业是文体娱、水利环境和农林牧渔业。制造业行业分类下，固投增速上行较多的行业是计算
机、通信和其他电子设备制造业、化学原料及化学制品制造业和电气机械及器材制造业，固投增速
下行较多的行业是纺织业、金属制品业和有色金属冶炼及压延加工业 。 
据统计局解读，1-9 月知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.3%，高技术产业投资同比增长 4.9%，其中信息
服务业，航空、航天器及设备制造业，电子及通信设备制造业投资分别增长 20.2%、12.5%、7.6%。

累计同比下降 13.9%。1-9 月商品房待售面积同比下降 1.5%，其中住宅面积同比下降 0.7%，办公楼
同比下降 0.6%，商业地产同比下降 5.7%。从房地产投资资金来源看，1-9 月国内贷款占比 13.4%，
较 1-8 月下降 0.1 个百分点，个人按揭贷款占比 13.5%，较 1-8 月上升 0.1 个百分点，定金预收款占
比 31.7%，较 1-8 月上升 0.1 个百分点，自筹资金占比 36.2%，较 1-8 月持平。从增速看，1-9 月房地
产开发资金来源累计同比增速下降 21.6%， 其中国内贷款资金来源同比下降33.1%， 定金和预收款增
速下降 14.8%，个人按揭贷款资金来源增速下降 22.1%。2026 年 9 月，70 个大中城市新建商品住宅
价格指数同比下降 3.0%，较 8 月上升 0.3 个百分点，二手住宅价格指数下降 4.8%，较 8 月上升 0.3
个百分点。

### Page 11

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 11

从居民收入来源来看，2026 年前三季度工资性收入同比增速 5.3%，经营净收入增速 6.6%，财产净
收入累计同比增速 1.6%，转移净收入增速 5.7%；从结构来看，工资性收入占比 57.4%，较 2026 年
上半年下降 0.5 个百分点，经营净收入占比上升 0.4 个百分点，财产净收入占比下降 0.2 个百分点，
转移净收入占比上升 0.3 个百分点。

图表 30. 全国居民人均收入和增速  图表 31. 居民收入来源增速

资料来源：万得，中银证券  资料来源：万得，中银证券

2026 年前三季度居民的边际消费倾向较上半年上升。2026 年前三季度居民人均消费支出 22354 元，
同比增长 2.7%，扣除价格因素，实际增速增长 2.7%，与 2026 年上半年相比，居民消费增速持平。
2026 年前三季度，居民消费八大分类中，同比增速较高的是其他用品及服务、生活用品及服务、教
育文化娱乐和食品烟酒，与 2026 年上半年相比，增速上升超过 1.0 个百分点的仅有医疗保健；2026
年前三季度 居民消费支出结构中占比较高的 还是食品烟酒（ 29.7%）、居住（ 21.3%）和交通通信
（14.3%），与 2026 年上半年相比，占比上升超过 1.0 个百分点的是教育文化娱乐，与 2025 年同期
相比，占比上升的是食品烟酒、生活用品及服务、教育文化娱乐和其他用品及服务 ，占比下降的是
居住、交通通信和医疗保健。2026 年前三季度居民边际消费倾向较上半年小幅上升，但整体来看居
民消费意愿较弱，边际消费倾向和增量收入边际消费倾向对比 2025 年同期均有所下降， 特别是增量
收入边际消费倾向降幅较大。

### Page 12

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 12

结论：内需出现向好迹象 
三季度经济增速超预期，全年稳增长压力下降 。三季度实际 GDP 同比增长 4.5%超出市场预期，带
动前三季度实际 GDP 累计同比增速达到 4.6%，略高于全年增速目标区间下限，加之去年四季度经
济增长基数偏低，一定程度上缓解了短期稳增长的压力。从 9 月经济数据看，稳增长的政策起到了
一定效果：工业增加值同比增速符合市场预期，同时维持在较高增速上；社零同比增速较 8 月明显
上升，表明内需仍有平稳释放的潜力；固定资产投资累计同比降幅持平于 1-8 月，但从我们计算的
当月增速看，基建投资和制造业投资增速均较 8 月出现较大幅度好转。从季节规律看，四季度经济
增长仍有支撑：一是生产端补库存，可能驱动工业增加值同比增速维持在当前的增速水平，考虑到
PPI 同比增速走高和 9 月出口交货值增速走高，价格因素和外需可能成为四季度生产端超预期的推
动力；二是四季度是传统消费旺季，从社零环比增速看，在 9 月环比高增长的基础上，四季度的三
个月在电商促销和春节前消费等因素推动下，环比仍能 维持较强的正增长；三是四季度也是海外消
费旺季，进出口或依赖我国生产端优势，持续受益于外需拉动。 
经济向“新”带动政策求“变”。 8 月底至今，从稳增长的角度出发，政策做了多方面调整：一是
国家发改委等部门召开全国投资工作推进会议，部署七项稳投资 工作，二是住建部等三部门推进商
品住房销售制度改革，稳定地产预期，三是证监会发文支持构建房地产发展新模式，四是央行调整
完善几项货币政策工具稳增长， 五是财政部安排使用地方政府债务结存限额5500 亿元， 六是中共中
央、国务院印发《关于发展新质生产力的意见》，继续大力支持经济转型升级等等。我们认为虽然
2026 年年内稳增长的压力减轻，但从中长期看，经济增长的挑战依然很大：从全球范围内看，包括
但不限于 AI 技术发展对生产关系的冲击、地缘局势变化对能源和通胀的影响、经济增长 K 型分化
在行业之间和国别之间带来的发展不均衡、各经济体面临的增长挑战与财政压力、发达国家货币政
策收缩与金融条件收紧对全球资本市场的外溢性影响等等；从国内看，我们面临的挑战依然是以维
持经济向新向优趋势和供强需弱的现实矛盾为主。 我们认为未来一段时间内，我国宏观经济的主要
表现或仍是增速缓步下台阶，因此积极的财政政策和适度宽松的货币政策发力重点或更多聚焦于托
底民生和实现社会公平，其次是发展先进生产力，然后才是稳增长。 尽管国内经济看起来只是“平
稳”，但我们认为相较于其他经济体，我国的比较优势依然非常明显 。

风险提示：全球通胀二次上行；欧美经济 超预期回落；国际局势复杂化。

### Page 13

2026 年 10 月 19 日 9 月和三季度经济数据点评 13

评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准：  
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

沪深市场基准指数为沪深 300 指数；新三板市场基准指数为三板成指或三板做市指数；香港市场基准指数为恒生指数
或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

## Document 228: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 241: AI帮储户多赚钱，银行却慌了：花旗、美银、摩根大通集体对AI Agent设防

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI帮储户多赚钱，银行却慌了：花旗、美银、摩根大通集体对AI Agent设防.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

花旗、美银与摩根⼤通相继限制 AI 代理访问客⼾账⼾并代⾏交易，主因系担忧 AI ⼯具⾃动
将资⾦转⾄⾼息产品，威胁数万亿美元低成本存款。⽬前交易责任认定仍属空⽩，美联储
监管层私下对此表⽰⽀持。银⾏业正通过技术设防与提升存款黏性，应对 AI 引发的利润挑
战。
摘要：
花旗、美国银⾏和摩根⼤通相继宣布限制 AI 智能代理访问客⼾账⼾，这场集体设防的背后，是银
⾏业对数万亿美元低成本存款可能被 AI" 扫⾛ " 的深层焦虑。
花旗明确表⽰，不允许 " 第三⽅ AI 代理或助⼿登录客⼾账⼾，或直接在其数字银⾏平台上进⾏交
易 " ，称此举旨在保障客⼾账⼾与信息安全。美国银⾏⾸席执⾏官 Brian Moynihan 本周三在接受
CNBC 采访时表⽰，该⾏正在限制 AI 代理访问消费者存款账⼾，同时研究在 AI 发起⽀付指令时如
何界定责任主体。摩根⼤通⾸席执⾏官 Jamie Dimon 则在该⾏三季度业绩电话会议上表⽰，将限
制 AI 代理访问客⼾账⼾，理由是 " 你不会希望出现流氓代理，⽽且这类代理可能数以千计 " 。

与此同时，华尔街⾼管已就消费者使⽤ AI 代理进⾏⾦融交易的快速增⻓，与美联储监管⼈员展开
沟通；监管层私下对银⾏封堵 AI 代理访问客⼾账⼾的举措持欢迎态度。
存款流失威胁利润根基
此次银⾏业集体⾏动的直接导⽕索，是 AI 个⼈理财⼯具的加速涌现。据英国《⾦融时报》报道，
Meta 于 9 ⽉推出 Muse AI 个⼈助⼿后，业界担忧情绪明显升温 —— 市场普遍担⼼ AI 代理将⾃动替
消费者将闲置资⾦从银⾏账⼾转移⾄收益更⾼的产品。
阿波罗⾸席经济学家 Torsten Slok 在⼀份报告中将这⼀⻛险量化： AI 助⼿可能⾃动将家庭闲置资
⾦从全国平均仅 0.1% 的活期账⼾利率，转移⾄利率达 3.3% ⾄ 5% 的账⼾。报告警告称， " 如果每
个家庭都使⽤ AI 代理优化现⾦余额收益，银⾏可能损失⼤量⽤于发放贷款的低成本存款，这将对
整个⾦融体系构成威胁。 "
摩根⼤通、美国银⾏和富国银⾏⽬前合计持有 1.6 万亿美元的⽆息存款，占其负债总额的 16% 。
若被迫对这部分存款⽀付 3% 的利率，三家银⾏每年将额外承担约 470 亿美元的成本 —— 相当于

其年度税后净利润总和的近⼀半。
各⾏表态与责任认定困境
在设防⽅式上，各⾏有所差异，但核⼼逻辑⼀致：在责任归属尚不明确之前，不允许 AI 代理⾃主
操作账⼾资⾦。
Brian Moynihan 明确表⽰， " 我们强烈认为，账⼾转账授权应有⼈⼯参与。 " 他表⽰，美国银⾏正
与 AI 代理服务商沟通，提⽰其注意消费者可能事后对资⾦去向产⽣异议的⻛险。⼀位知情⼈⼠证
实，美国银⾏已限制 AI 代理的相关访问权限。
Jamie Dimon 则着重强调⻛险管控逻辑：
" 我们必须以正确的⽅式保护客⼾。我们正在构建相应⼯具。在此之前，我们不会允许任何外部
代理进⼊公司系统，因为这对客⼾和摩根⼤通⽽⾔⻛险太⼤。 "
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
花旗表⽰，客⼾仍可通过特定开放银⾏服务商授权对账⼾信息进⾏有限的只读访问。
据知情⼈⼠透露，针对 AI 代理在⾦融交易中的合规边界，美联储已与银⾏业⾼管展开私下沟通，
但美联储拒绝就此置评。
监管层私下对银⾏限制 AI 代理访问账⼾的举措持⽀持态度，官员认为，消费者如何授权⾃主技术
代其完成⾦融交易、以及出现问题后的责任认定，可能需要通过⽴法层⾯加以厘清。
银⾏如何应对：让存款 " 更难流⾛ "
⾯对 AI 带来的存款竞争压⼒，银⾏业已开始寻求应对之策。咨询公司 Simon-Kucher ⾼级合伙⼈
Christoph Stegmeier 表⽰，银⾏需要通过扩⼤客⼾忠诚度计划、优化贷款利率等⽅式，提升存
款产品的黏性。
" 你必须让你的存款产品更难被替代， " 他说。

这场由 AI 驱动的存款争夺战，才刚刚开始。银⾏在技术防线上能坚守多久，以及监管框架能否跟
上 AI 代理的演进速度，将成为决定这场博弈⾛向的两⼤关键变量。

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years. 1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

4 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.
MSCI Emerging Markets c is a stock market index that tracks the performance of large-cap and mid-cap companies across 24 developing and fast-growing nations.
MSCI All-Country World Index ex USA is a market-capitalization-weighted stock market index that tracks large- and mid-cap equity performance across developed and emerging markets

worldwide, excluding the United States.
MSCI Europe, Australasia, and the Far East (EAFE) Index is a stock market index that measures the performance of large- and mid-cap companies across 21 developed.
Dow Jones Industrial Average Index is a stock market index that tracks 30 major public companies listed on the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) and Nasdaq.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is an alternative version of the standard S&P 500 that gives every constituent company an identical weight of 0.2% during each quarterly rebalance, rather

than weighting companies by their market capitalization.
Russell 2000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of approximately 2,000 small-cap (small-capitalization) public companies in the United States.
S&P 500 sub-sectors and industry groups Global Industry Classification Standard (GICS®)/S&P 500 Total Return Index, including Information Technology Total Return (TR) USD;
Consumer Discretionary TR USD; Industrials TR USD; Real Estate TR USD; Communication Services TR USD; Materials  TR USD; Financials TR USD; Consumer Staples TR USD; Utilities TR USD;

Investing involves risk, including the possible loss of principal. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.
Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.

## Document 318: Fable 5.5要来了？报道称Anthropic正开发新模型，主打_意图推断_能力

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fable 5.5要来了？报道称Anthropic正开发新模型，主打_意图推断_能力.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据 The Information 报道， Anthropic 正开发⼀款能主动推断⽤⼾模糊指令真实意图的新模
型，具备更强推理与创造性问题解决能⼒。分析认为若模糊指令推理能⼒落地，⽤⼾与 AI
助⼿的交互⽅式将改变，⽤⼾不再需要精⼼设计提⽰词，模型将承担更多 " 揣摩意图 " 的⼯
作。
摘要：
Anthropic 正在开发⼀款能够主动推断⽤⼾模糊指令背后真实意图的新模型， AI 助⼿的产品竞争
焦点，正从 " 听懂并执⾏指令 " 转向 " 理解意图 " 。
10 ⽉ 19 ⽇，据 The Information 报道，⼀位使⽤过该模型的⼈⼠透露，这款尚未发布的模型在⽤
⼾指令含糊不清时，仍能推断出⽤⼾试图完成的⽬标，并展现出更强的推理能⼒和更具创造性
的问题解决能⼒。
报道未披露模型名称、定价或上下⽂窗⼝等技术参数。同⽇， The Information 联合创始⼈ Amir
Efrati 在 X 平台发⽂称：
截⾄ 10 ⽉ 20 ⽇， Anthropic 官⽅模型⻚⾯仍仅列出四款现役模型： Claude Fable 5.1 、 Claude

Opus 5.5 、 Claude Sonnet 5.5 与 Claude Haiku 5.5 。 Anthropic ⾃ 10 ⽉ 7 ⽇发布 Haiku 5.5 以来，
未再发布任何模型公告。
模糊指令下的 " 意图推断 "
当前主流⼤模型⾯对模糊指令，通常倾向于要求⽤⼾澄清或给出保守的通⽤回答。
Anthropic 新模型的关键差异在于，它在指令不完整、不精确时，主动推断⽤⼾最可能的真实⽬
标并据此⾏动。据报道援引⼀位使⽤过该模型的⼈⼠描述，该模型具备更强的推理能⼒和更具
创造性的问题解决能⼒。
若模糊指令推理能⼒落地，⽤⼾与 AI 助⼿的交互⽅式将改变：⽤⼾不再需要精⼼设计提⽰词，
模型将承担更多 " 揣摩意图 " 的⼯作。
在编程、数据分析等复杂任务场景中，⽤⼾的初始需求往往并不完整，⼀款能推断意图的模型
可以在⽤⼾只给出粗略⽬标时，⾃主拆解任务、补⻬缺失信息并交付结果。
新模型的发布时间表与落地节奏尚未公布，其能否真正改写  AI 助⼿的交互范式仍有待观察。

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

### Page 8

8
內部參考文件請勿外流
► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

預估今明年產業獲利差異，醫療保健和生技成長最大

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 防禦搭配科技股，以槓鈴策略穩中求勝!

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 409: The Wrap on Asia_ Asia’s goods disinflation over_ (2026-10-20)

### Page 1

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WRAP ON ASIA
Mapping out Asia ...
Illiana Jain
Economist, Westpac Group
P: +61 403  908 032
E: illiana.jain@westpac.com.au
• For two decades, lower labour costs across Asia helped
suppress global goods inflation.
• Much of that disinflationary pulse originated in North Asia,

where production was increasingly offshored to lower-cost
labour markets.
• Today, ageing populations and rising incomes are lifting
labour costs across much of the region.
• While production continues to migrate towards South
and Southeast Asia, the next phase of manufacturing is
increasingly driven by capital, technology and power rather
than low-cost labour.
• As a result, labour costs are unlikely to provide the same
disinflationary impulse that supported goods prices over the

previous decade.
• Technology has historically provided a source of disinflation
as production costs fell and competition intensified.
• Asia sits at the centre of the AI supply chain through firms
such as TSMC, Samsung and SK Hynix, which produce
critical semiconductors and memory chips used in AI
infrastructure.
• Rising costs for electricity and critical minerals are increasing
production costs across the sector.
• Strong demand has allowed firms to pass these costs on

while maintaining healthy profitability. SK Hynix’s net profit
rose from ₩19.8tn to ₩42.9tn (+117%yr).
• Firms are also competing on performance and technological
capability rather than price, allowing technology-related
inflation to emerge for the first time in years.
• Many Asian economies remain reliant on imported energy,
leaving producers exposed to higher oil and LNG costs.
• Semiconductor manufacturing and the broader AI
infrastructure build-out are highly energy intensive.

Expansions by TSMC, Samsung and SK Hynix are increasing
electricity demand across North Asia while data-centre
investment adds a further source of demand.
• As technology firms compete with traditional manufacturers
for power, energy costs are becoming an increasingly
important part of the production process.
• Rising production costs are therefore being driven by both
higher energy prices and stronger underlying demand for
electricity.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WRAP ON ASIA
• Chinese producer-price deflation remains evident across
parts of traditional manufacturing, particularly sectors
facing persistent overcapacity such as automobiles
• Historically, Chinese producer prices acted as the pricing
anchor for much of Asia’s manufacturing sector.

• What has changed is that parts of the region are increasingly
specialising in industries where China is not the dominant
supplier.
• Strong demand for semiconductors, memory and AI-related
goods is allowing firms in Taiwan and South Korea to set
prices according to their own market conditions despite
ongoing weakness in Chinese producer prices.
• China’s deflationary pulse remains important, but it no
longer guarantees lower prices across the rest of Asia.

• Rising costs were previously offset by production shifting to
lower-cost economies elsewhere in Asia.
• Production continues to migrate towards Vietnam, India,
Indonesia, Malaysia and Thailand, helping contain costs.
• However, these economies are unlikely to replicate the scale
of China’s integration into global manufacturing during the
2000s.
• High-value production remains concentrated in North
Asia while firms increasingly value resilience, security and

specialisation alongside cost minimisation.
• Future supply-chain shifts may therefore help limit
cost pressures, but are unlikely to generate the same
disinflationary pulse seen over the last two decades.
• For much of the past decade, falling goods prices helped
offset elevated services inflation across developed markets.
The forces that drove that disinflation are now becoming
less powerful.
• While Chinese deflation and production relocation to lower-

cost economies will continue to provide some support, they
are unlikely to fully offset these pressures.
• As a result, imported goods may provide less assistance in
bringing inflation back to target than they have in the past.
• This leaves central banks increasingly reliant on containing
domestic inflation pressures, particularly in services.
• As a result, achieving inflation targets may require a longer
period of restrictive monetary policy than has historically

been the case.

## Document 423: United States _ A shock-resistant expansion (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/United States _ A shock-resistant expansion (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
A SHOCK-RESISTANT EXPANSION
US growth remains resilient despite successive shocks. According to our forecasts, it is expected to reach 2.2% in 2026 and
2.3% in 2027. Its main driver is still investment in artificial intelligence (AI), which has remained entirely unaffected by the
energy crisis. The labour market has seen a marked improvement in job creation and a fall in the unemployment rate. Core

inflation remains stubbornly high and above the Federal Reserve (Fed)’s target. Against this backdrop, the Fed has begun a
hiking cycle in September, which, according to our scenario, would bring the Fed Funds target to 4.50% (upper limit) by Q1
2027. At the same time, long-term rates are under upward pressure.
1  This includes: energy and communications; data centres (facilities); IT equipment and peripherals; communication equipment (equipment); software; and research and develop-

ment (intellectual-property products).
THE ENERGY SHOCK IS NOT HOLDING BACK GROWTH
Driven by AI-related investment, US growth is holding up despite ri -
sing energy inflation. We forecast GDP growth at 2.2% in 2026 and
2.3% in 2027 on an annual average basis. The AI-related investment
cycle1 (+14.8% y/y in H1 2026) is expected to continue, accompanied by
potential productivity gains. However, other private-investment seg -
ments (including residential property) remain sluggish. In addition, the

rising cost of capital could accentuate its ‘K-shaped’ profile. Household
consumption is expected, on the whole, to benefit from the rebound in
job creation and to remain above 2% y/y through to the end of 2027.
In H1 2026, it held up against resurging inflation due to an average
year-on-year increase of 11.5% in tax refunds and a savings rate stan-
ding at its lowest level since 2022 (2.8% in Q2).
RECORD CAPITAL-RAISING DRIVEN BY AI
This year, AI-related investments are driving massive capital-raising

by companies through loans, bond issues or share placements. This
trend is set to continue given the seemingly colossal scale of the fun -
ding requirements, even as risk premia in the bond market begin to
widen. According to their statements, the five tech giants could invest
nearly USD 900 bn this year and over USD 1,000 bn next year—more
than double the figure for 2025. Spurred on by regulatory easing, the
major US banks have announced that they will mobilise significant re-

sources to support projects considered strategic for the United States
(AI, defence, energy and infrastructure). While funding for the AI boom
appears unaffected by rising long-term interest rates, these latter are
likely to continue to weigh on household demand for mortgages. Since
the start of the conflict in the Middle East, 30-year mortgage rates
have risen by 78 bp (to 6.76% at the start of September). Conversely,
consumer lending remained buoyant in H1 2026 and is supporting

household spending.
TWO-SPEED GROWTH IN INDUSTRY
Industrial production rebounded slightly in 2025 (+1.1%), driven by AI
and aerospace. We forecast an acceleration to +1.5% in 2026 and +2%
in 2027. Investment in AI is bolstering the electronics sector, while the
(civil and military) aerospace sector is rebounding as order backlogs
are cleared and defence requirements rise. Conversely, sectors ex -
posed to rising tariffs (in particular, the steel, aluminium and auto -

motive sectors) are still on the back burner, contrary to the objective
of these measures. This positive momentum in industrial production,
while concentrated, is expected to continue, bolstered by the recove -
ry in business sentiment. The ISM Manufacturing Index has been in
expansionary territory since early 2026, despite ongoing pressures on
prices and supply chains.

world
LABOUR MARKET: A LOW UNEMPLOYMENT RATE ON A DOWNWARD
TREND
Job creation (non-farm payrolls) has rebounded, averaging +80,000 per
month since the start of the year, compared with +10,000 in 2025. This
growth is no longer limited to the healthcare sector. Excluding health-
care, job creation is up by an average of +38,000 in 2026, compared

with -47,000 in 2025. Manufacturing and construction, two sectors
driven by AI, are contributing to this rebound. At the same time, the
labour force has not grown since January 2025 due to the decline in the
foreign-born workforce (down 4.2% cumulatively over the period). Ex -
cluding the impact of COVID-19, the labour force participation rate is at
its lowest level since the 1970s, standing at around 61.5%. The rebound
in labour demand and persistent pressure on the labour supply have

brought the unemployment rate down to 4.1% in August 2026 (a 0.3 pp
fall since the start of the year). The jobless rate is expected to fall to
3.9% at the end of 2027 (4.2% corresponds to full employment, accor -
ding to the CBO) thanks to a job creation rate of around 40,000–60,000
per month.
INFLATION: STRUCTURALLY ABOVE TARGET
The rise in oil prices has triggered a rebound in CPI inflation. The
contribution from the energy component (+1.2 pp) largely accounts for

the rise in inflation since February. A slowdown was seen (from +4.2%
to +3.4% y/y) between the May peak and August. This is expected to
continue due to base effects on energy prices. We anticipate the trough
to be +1.8% y/y in Q2 2027 before a stabilisation at around +2.3% y/y
later in the year.
Meanwhile, core inflation is expected to remain above the Fed’s 2%
target, and above +2.5% y/y in 2027. This highlights the structural
nature of inflation, beyond short-term fluctuations linked to energy.

Non-housing services are expected to show significant inertia, with in-
flation hovering at around +3% y/y by the end of 2027. The potential for
disinflation also appears limited for housing-related services, as the
rise in rents shows no signs of abating. Finally, inflation on non-energy
goods is slowing as the effects of tariffs fade. However, the AI cycle is
exerting upward pressure through the general rise in the cost of elec -
tronic goods.
FED: GREEN LIGHT FOR RATE HIKES

The FOMC has embarked on a cycle of rate increases. The Fed Funds
target was raised to 4.0% (+25 bp) at the September meeting, and we
anticipate two further rises that would bring the policy rate to 4.5% in
January 2027. These three rises (made possible by reassuring develop-
ments in employment and economic activity) are intended to reverse
the three cuts in 2025 adopted to counter the ‘downside risks’ to the
labour market at that time. As a result, the committee can recalibrate

its policy towards ‘price stability’ and, through its interest rate deci -
sions, reflect the priority given to this objective in its communications.
The risks point towards further monetary tightening: in our baseline
scenario, core inflation would remain sticky and the fall in unemploy -
ment would give the FOMC room for manoeuvre to raise rates further.
PUBLIC FINANCES: UNDERLYING DETERIORATION AND PRESSURE ON
LONG- TERM RATES
According to our estimates, the federal deficit is set to widen by 0.7 pp

to -6.6% of GDP in 2026 (by 0.4 pp to -3.1% for the primary deficit),
weighed down by falling tariff revenue (refunds of tariffs already col -
lected and lower-than-expected receipts). In 2027, the end of this one-
off effect is expected to lead to a marginal improvement to -6.3% (to
-2.8% for the primary deficit). The growing burden of interest payments
(3.5% of GDP in 2026 and 3.6% in 2027, following 3.2% in 2025) and
the conflict in Iran (affecting the military budget) are weighing on the

budget balance and federal-debt dynamics. Federal debt is still on an
upward trend, with no particular change in direction, exceeding 100% of
GDP (101.9%, up 3.3 pp, in 2026, and 103.5% in 2027). It is expected to
reach 110% by the end of the decade against a backdrop of budget de-
ficits of close to 6% of GDP , assuming fiscal policy remains unchanged.
Furthermore, the mid-term elections (which see the entire House of
Representatives and one-third of the Senate up for re-election) could

result in a divided Congress or in both chambers changing hands to
the Democratic Party. Should the former occur, this would increase the
risk of a legislative deadlock, while the presidential veto would limit
the Democrats’ ability to influence fiscal policy in the latter scenario.
Against this backdrop, pressure on the bond market is likely to continue
in the coming months. Treasury issuance is rising, fuelled by increa -
sing spending requirements (in areas such as AI, defence and supply

chains). At the same time, shifts in investor demographics are increa-
sing demand volatility, and issuances by companies in the artificial
intelligence sector are intensifying competition for capital. We antici -
pate a 10-year yield of 4.95% in Q1 2027, before a slight easing brought
about by Fed rate rises, which would lend credibility to the price-sta -
bility objective. However, according to our forecast, 10-year yield is
expected to remain close to a 20-year high, at 4.80% in Q4 2027.

THE US RELIES ON IMPORTS FOR ITS GROWTH
The US trade deficit has recently started to rise again. According to
our forecasts, it is expected to account for the same proportion of GDP
in 2026 as it did in 2024 (4.4%). This trend is driven by imports of
AI-related capital goods, which are set to rise from 1.2% to 2.1% of GDP
between 2022 and 2026. Investment in AI is, in fact, import-intensive
(Chart 2), which dampens its positive impact on growth and highlights

the US’s dependence on foreign supplies in a cycle that it is neverthe-
less spearheading.
The additional tariffs have failed to reverse the trend in the trade de -
ficit. Furthermore, the IEEPA tariffs struck down by the Supreme Court
were replaced, in July 2026, by ‘Section 301’ tariffs (forced labour) ran-
ging from 10% to 12.5%. These tariffs target 60 countries and have
been introduced on top of existing sector-specific tariffs (steel, alumi -

nium and automotive). Following these adjustments, the statutory rate
(measured against the 2024 import structure) remained slightly above
10%. The main risks relate to potential new sector-specific tariffs and
the renegotiation of the USMCA.
Article completed on 17 September 2026
Anis Bensaidani

Céline Choulet
celine.choulet@bnpparibas.com

歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會
1
Thematic Report
重點提示
中東地緣衝突自2026年2月底爆發以來仍陷僵局，能源價格維
持高檔的時間較預期更長，令歐元區通膨壓力與升息預期升溫。
另一方面，歐洲政府擴大財政支出，公債供給增加，加上歐洲
央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價，令歐洲公債殖利率大幅走升，
德國十年期公債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月以來新高。
本報告透過問答，分析歐洲公債殖利率走高的原因、主權債務
與財政風險、政府因應措施、高利率對企業的影響，以及高利
率環境下的歐洲股債展望。
◼ 本波歐洲公債殖利率走高的驅動因素：能源價格上漲推升通
膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，同時，財政支出令歐洲公

債供給增加與歐央持續縮表使私人部門需要吸收更多公債供
給，推升期限溢價，財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外
壓力，加上全球利率上升的外溢影響，共同推升殖利率走高。
◼ 歐洲政府調整發債結構，央行備有穩市工具：歐洲政府縮短
發債期限以控制融資成本，預估此趨勢將延續至2027年；
萬一主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲央行有OMT與TPI政策
工具，但不會輕易動用。
◼ 整體歐洲債務風險尚可控但中長期壓力持續累積：目前利率
水準尚未對整體債務可持續性構成普遍威脅，但財政脆弱性
較高的國家壓力升高，法國尤其需要關注。財政與政治變數
使法國資產風險溢價維持高位，目前股債定價已反映相當程
度的負面風險。
◼ 德國公債殖利率短期將維持高檔：歐洲央行12月仍有再升息
可能，待能源價格與通膨壓力緩和、升息預期降溫後，利率
才有回落空間，但難降至3%以下。
◼ 歐洲企業資產負債表健康、獲利具韌性，緩衝高利率衝擊。
◼ 高利率限制歐股估值擴張，歐股維持中立，聚焦獲利成長與
政策支撐的產業：銀行受惠於目前利率環境與信貸需求，工

業、電氣化/能源安全及AI受惠戰略自主與資本支出提升。

Q2：面對公債殖利率上升，歐洲政府如何因應？
面對公債殖利率上升 ，歐洲政府調整發債結構 、增加短年期債
券發行，以控制高利率環境下的融資成本 ，預期此趨勢將延續
至2027年；惟縮短債務期限雖可降低當期融資成本，也將提高
中期再融資風險。
Q3：萬一歐洲主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲有哪些
政策工具可以運用？
歐洲央行主要政策工具有二 ，分別為直接貨幣交易 (OMT)與傳
導保護工具(TPI)。
(1)直接貨幣交易(OMT)：2012年9月推出，源自歐債危機，可
在次級市場購買符合條件國家的1至3年期政府公債，且購買
規模事前不設上限 ，惟須搭配歐洲穩定機制 (ESM)的金融援
助計畫，並具嚴格的附帶條件，使用門檻較高，由主權國家

主動申請。
(2)傳導保護工具 (TPI)：2022年7月推出 ，可在次級市場購買
符合條件國家的 1至10年期政府部門債券 ，目的在於因應與
該國基本面不相稱的無序市場波動 ，避免金融市場分裂影響
貨幣政策傳導。啟動時將評估該國是否符合歐盟財政規範 、
債務是否具備可持續性等條件 ，由歐央評估後決定是否啟動。
不過，OMT與TPI均屬市場失序時的最後手段 ，並非用於壓低
正常的主權債殖利率或消除國家間的基本面利差 ，啟動有嚴格
條件，歐央不會輕易動用，目前此兩項工具均未實際動用過。
高利率下，歐洲政府調整發債、央行備有穩市工具

Q4：歐洲政府債務的可持續性是否有立即風險？
主要歐洲國家除德國與西班牙外 ，IMF預估2026年政府債務佔
GDP比重均超過 100%。同時，近年地緣衝突頻率升高 ，戰略
自主與強化地區安全已成為歐洲核心目標 。為了讓成員國有擴
大國防支出的空間 ，歐盟允許成員國啟動 國家豁免條款，得以
在增加國防支出的同時 ，暫時不受歐盟財政規則約束 ，截至目
前已有18個歐盟會員國申請。國防支出提升，若未搭配增加財
政收入或其他支出調整，將使政府債務維持在較高路徑。
所幸，從利率與經濟成長率差距來看 ，目前利率水準尚未對歐
洲整體債務可持續性構成 立即威脅，但部分高負債國家的債務
壓力仍須留意。過去十年低利率環境的效益逐步消退 ，利率與

經濟成長率的差距 (r－g)較過去不利 ，加上期限溢價回升 ，使
高負債國家的債務動態面臨風險。
檢視歐元區四大經濟體 ，德國、西班牙政府債務的利率低於經
濟成長率，債務動態相對穩健；義大利政府債務 佔GDP比重最
高，所幸在政府控制支出下，自2024年以來維持初級財政盈餘，
有助控制債務比率進一步擴大。法國利率雖略低於經濟成長率 ，
但政府債務已接近 GDP的120%，在財政赤字偏高 、利息支出
持續增加且財政整頓進展有限 的情況下，未來兩年債務佔GDP
比率預期將持續上升，財政脆弱性相對較高。
整體歐洲債務風險可控但中長期壓力正在累積

Q5：近期法德利差顯著擴大，驅動因素與對法國股債
市的影響？
財政與政治不確定性升溫 ，推升法國主權風險溢價 ：法國步入
財政與政治不確定性升溫時期 ，不利的財政前景 ，加上4Q26
預算協商與2027年將舉行總統大選，政治不確定性升溫推升十
年期法德利差9月下旬突破100個基點，9/28升至112個基點、
創2012年6月以來新高。法國總統大選將於2027年4~5月舉行，
4/18舉行第一輪投票 ，若無候選人過半 ，5/2將舉行第二輪投
票。由於法國政治版圖高度分裂 、且極右派支持度領先 ，相較
於過往選舉周期，2027年法國選舉的不確定更高，預估將令市
場在2027年總統大選前維持謹慎情緒。
民調反映政治版圖分裂 ，增加財政政策的不確定性： 民調顯示，
極右派國民聯盟候選人瑪琳·勒龐在總統大選首輪的支持度領先，
超過三成，極左派法國不屈黨候選人梅朗雄 、中間偏右候選人
愛德華·菲利普民調落在15~18%左右。法國大選高機率將會進
入第二輪投票，取決於進入第二輪的候選人 ，目前民調顯示以

中間偏右候選人菲利普最有希望擊敗勒龐 ，若為極左派梅朗雄
進入第二輪，則勒龐勝選機率較高。

弱的法國財政增添另一層不確定性 。與以往政治週期不同 ，法
